PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CUBO_VAREJO_CARTOES_01 AS
SELECT A.*,
       B.MARCA_AP_VAREJO

FROM CUBO_VAREJO_CARTOES AS A
LEFT JOIN AP_CNPJ_VAREJO_01 AS B
ON A.CPF_PADRONIZADO = B.CPF_CNPJ_PADRONIZADO;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CUBO_VAREJO_DEMAIS_01 AS
SELECT A.*,
       B.MARCA_AP_VAREJO

FROM CUBO_VAREJO_DEMAIS AS A
LEFT JOIN AP_CONTRATO_VAREJO_01 AS B
ON A.NuObjetoNegociacao = B.NuObjetoNegociacao;
QUIT;


DATA AP_CUBO_VAREJO DEMAIS_CUBO;
SET CUBO_VAREJO_CARTOES_01
    CUBO_VAREJO_DEMAIS_01;

IF MARCA_AP_VAREJO = '1' THEN OUTPUT AP_CUBO_VAREJO;
ELSE OUTPUT DEMAIS_CUBO;

RUN;



DATA BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS;
SET AP_CUBO_VAREJO;
IF DataSource IN ('CARTOES' ) THEN OUTPUT BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES;
ELSE OUTPUT BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS;
RUN;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES_PROP AS
SELECT CPF_PADRONIZADO,
       SUM(VrSaldoUtilizado) AS VrSaldoUtilizado_SUM,
       SUM(VrPDDutilizada) AS VrPDDutilizada_SUM,
	   (CALCULATED VrPDDutilizada_SUM / CALCULATED VrSaldoUtilizado_SUM)  AS PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO
FROM BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES
GROUP BY 1;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES_02 AS
SELECT A.*,
       B.PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO
FROM BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES AS A
LEFT JOIN BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES_PROP AS B
ON A.CPF_PADRONIZADO = B.CPF_PADRONIZADO;
QUIT;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS_PROP AS
SELECT NuObjetoNegociacao,
       SUM(VrSaldoUtilizado) AS VrSaldoUtilizado_SUM,
       SUM(VrPDDutilizada) AS VrPDDutilizada_SUM,
	   (CALCULATED VrPDDutilizada_SUM / CALCULATED VrSaldoUtilizado_SUM)  AS PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO
FROM BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS
GROUP BY 1;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS_02 AS
SELECT A.*,
       B.PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO
FROM BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS AS A
LEFT JOIN BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS_PROP AS B
ON A.NuObjetoNegociacao = B.NuObjetoNegociacao;
QUIT;


DATA APEND_VAREJO_AP;
SET BASE_AP_VAREJO_CARTOES_02
    BASE_AP_VAREJO_DEMAIS_02;
RUN;



DATA APEND_VAREJO_AP_02;
SET APEND_VAREJO_AP;

IF PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO < 0.2 THEN FPR_AP = '93060150'; 
ELSE IF 0.2 <= PROP_PDD_AP_VAREJO < 0.5 THEN FPR_AP = '93020100'; 
ELSE FPR_AP = codigoFPR; 


codigoFPR = FPR_AP;
RUN;



DATA WORK.CUBO_4040_VAREJO_8;
SET APEND_VAREJO_AP_02
    DEMAIS_CUBO;
RUN;


DATA WORK.CUBO_4040_VAREJO_9;
SET WORK.CUBO_4040_VAREJO_8;


%MARCACAO_FPR(WORK.CUBO_4040_VAREJO_8)

RUN;

proc sql;
	create table work.base_aux_1 as
		select
			*
		from
			_PEPR_RA.BASECUBO&_DATA_REFERENCIA_._4040_VCarimbos
		where
			DataSource not in ('GCON2','CARTOES')
;
quit;

proc sql;
	create table work.base_aux_2 as
		select distinct
			NuObjetoNegociacao,
			codigoFPR
		from
			work.base_aux_1
		where
			missing(NuObjetoNegociacao) = 0
;
quit;

proc sql;
	create table work.base_aux_3 as
		select
			*
		from
			work.base_aux_2
		group by
			NuObjetoNegociacao
		having
			count(NuObjetoNegociacao) > 1
;
quit;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE RELATORIO_FPR_&_DATA_REFERENCIA_. AS
		select
			*
		from
			work.base_aux_1
		where
			NuObjetoNegociacao in (
				select distinct
					NuObjetoNegociacao
				from
					work.base_aux_3
			)
		order by
			NuObjetoNegociacao
;
quit;

proc sql;
	drop table work.base_aux_1;
	drop table work.base_aux_3;
	drop table work.base_aux_2;
quit;

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 030 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 298 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: 011 - Relatorio_Final */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-dF3hLeJmC3t9UZJh/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.Relatorio_FINAL AS
	SELECT
		t1.NuContaBACEN,
		t1.NuContaContabil,
		t1.NuContaPDD,
		t1.DataSource AS 'Visão de Origem'n,
		t1.DLO,
		t1.codigoSUBCONTA,
		t1.codigoFPR,
		t1.codigoMITIGADOR,
		t1.codigoCONVERSAO,
		t1.Tipo,
		t1.Fl_Acordo_Comp,
		t1.cambio,
		
		
		(SUM(t1.VrSaldoContaContabil)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS VrSaldoContaContabil,
		
		(SUM(t1.VrSaldoUtilizado)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS VrSaldoUtilizado,
		
		(SUM(t1.VrPDDutilizada)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS VrPDDutilizada,
		
		(SUM(t1.ValorExposicao)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS ValorExposicao,
		
		(SUM(t1.ExposicaoAposFatorConversao)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS ExposicaoAposFatorConvers,
		
		(SUM(t1.ValorEPR)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS ValorEPR,
		
		(SUM(t1.Capital)) FORMAT=COMMAX25.2 AS Capital
	FROM

_PEPR_RA.BASECUBO&_DATA_REFERENCIA_._4040_VCarimbos t1

		GROUP BY
			t1.NuContaBACEN,
			t1.NuContaContabil,
			t1.NuContaPDD,
			t1.DataSource,
			t1.DLO,
			t1.codigoSUBCONTA,
			t1.codigoFPR,
			t1.codigoMITIGADOR,
			t1.codigoCONVERSAO,
			t1.Tipo,
			t1.Fl_Acordo_Comp,
			t1.cambio;
QUIT;

		CREATE TABLE CADOC AS

QUIT;

/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_DUP AS
		SELECT * ,
				COUNT(NuObjetoNegociacao) as Contador_Contratos
		FROM WORK.RM_CLASS_TIT
		GROUP BY 1,2
		ORDER BY 1,2;
QUIT;


DATA RM_HTM
	 RM_AVS
	 RM_HFT
	 RM_VERIFICAR
	 RM_Contratos;

	 SET WORK.RM_CLASS_TIT;

	 OUTPUT RM_Contratos;

	 IF Classif_Titulos = "HTM" THEN OUTPUT RM_HTM;
	 ELSE IF Classif_Titulos = "AVS" THEN OUTPUT RM_AVS;
	 ELSE IF Classif_Titulos = "HFT" THEN OUTPUT RM_HFT;
	 ELSE OUTPUT RM_VERIFICAR;

RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE WORK.Contratos_Final AS
		SELECT 	NuObjetoNegociacao,
				CdVeiculoLegal,
				SUM(VrTitulos) AS VrTitulos
		FROM WORK.RM_CONTRATOS
		GROUP BY 1,2;
QUIT;


PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE WORK.RM_TABELA_TITULOS AS
	SELECT A.*,
		   B.Classif_Titulos as Class_HTM,
		   B.VrTitulos/A.VrTitulos as Perc_HTM,
		   C.Classif_Titulos as Class_AVS,
		   C.VrTitulos/A.VrTitulos as Perc_AVS,
		   D.Classif_Titulos as Class_HFT,
		   D.VrTitulos/A.VrTitulos as Perc_HFT
	FROM WORK.Contratos_Final AS A
		 LEFT JOIN WORK.RM_HTM AS B ON (A.NuObjetoNegociacao = B.NuObjetoNegociacao AND A.CdVeiculoLegal = B.CdVeiculoLegal)
		 LEFT JOIN WORK.RM_AVS AS C ON (A.NuObjetoNegociacao = C.NuObjetoNegociacao AND A.CdVeiculoLegal = C.CdVeiculoLegal)
		 LEFT JOIN WORK.RM_HFT AS D ON (A.NuObjetoNegociacao = D.NuObjetoNegociacao AND A.CdVeiculoLegal = D.CdVeiculoLegal);
;QUIT;

DATA _AUXCUBO.RM_CLASS_TITULOS_&_DATA_REFERENCIA_._V1(DROP=Class_AVS Class_HTM Class_HFT VrTitulos);
	SET WORK.RM_TABELA_TITULOS;

	IF MISSING(Class_HTM) = 0 AND MISSING(Class_AVS) = 0 AND MISSING(Class_HFT) = 0 THEN
		Class_Titulos = "AVS/HTM/HFT";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_HTM) = 0 AND MISSING(Class_AVS) = 0 THEN
		Class_Titulos = "HTM/AVS";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_AVS) = 0 AND MISSING(Class_HFT) = 0 THEN
		Class_Titulos = "AVS/HFT";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_HTM) = 0 AND MISSING(Class_HFT) = 0 THEN
		Class_Titulos = "HTM/HFT";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_HTM) = 0 THEN Class_Titulos = "HMT";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_AVS) = 0 THEN Class_Titulos = "AVS";

	ELSE IF MISSING(Class_HFT) = 0 THEN Class_Titulos = "HFT";

RUN;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 036 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */

DATA BASE_IF_04;
SET BASE_IF_03;

CodigoFPR_A_IF = CodigoFPR;
PzContratadoOperacao = intck('month', DtInicioOperacao, DtFimOperacao);


IF CAT_RISCO_IF = 'A' AND PzContratadoOperacao <= 3 THEN CodigoFPR = '22000020';
ELSE IF CAT_RISCO_IF = 'A' AND PzContratadoOperacao > 3 THEN CodigoFPR = '22060040';
ELSE IF CAT_RISCO_IF = 'B' AND PzContratadoOperacao <= 3 THEN CodigoFPR = '22100050';
ELSE IF CAT_RISCO_IF = 'B' AND PzContratadoOperacao > 3 THEN CodigoFPR = '22150075';
ELSE IF CAT_RISCO_IF = 'C' THEN CodigoFPR = '22170150';
ELSE CodigoFPR = ''; 


IF DataSource = 'CADOC4040' AND CAT_RISCO_IF = 'A' AND NuContaContabil IN ('1261010000000000004000000'
                                                                           '1261010000000000003000000' 
                                                                           '1261010000000000002000000'
                                                                           '1261010000000000001000000') THEN
CodigoFPR = '22000020';

IF DataSource = 'CADOC4040' AND CAT_RISCO_IF = 'A' AND NuContaContabil IN ('1884020000000000000000000'
                                                                           '1221001100002040001000000' 
                                                                           '1884015000000000000000000'
                                                                           '1221001100002040002000000'
                                                                           '1884090000000000000000000'


                                                                         
                                                                            '1432000000000000000000000'
                                                                            '1413090000001000000000000'
                                                                            '1414090000001000000000000'
                                                                            '1415010000002000001000000'
																			'1415010000003000001000000'
																			'1415030000001000001000000'
																			'1415030000001000002000000'
																			'1443000000001000000000000'
																			'1443000000002000000000000'
																			'1443000000003000000000000'
																			'1443000000004000000000000'
																			'1443000000005000000000000'
																			'1443000000006000000000000'
																			'1443000000007000000000000') THEN
CodigoFPR = '22060040';



IF CodigoFPR = '22000020' THEN DO;
  IF (CdVeiculoLegal = 14273) OR (SUBSTR(NuObjetoNegociacao,1,3) = '611') OR (CPF_CNPJ_ContraParte = '00000000000000')
  THEN CodigoFPR = '22010020';
END;

IF CodigoFPR = '22060040' THEN DO;
  IF (CdVeiculoLegal = 14273) OR (SUBSTR(NuObjetoNegociacao,1,3) = '611') OR (CPF_CNPJ_ContraParte = '00000000000000')
  THEN CodigoFPR = '22070040';
END;


IF CodigoFPR = '22100050' THEN DO;
  IF (CdVeiculoLegal = 14273) OR (SUBSTR(NuObjetoNegociacao,1,3) = '611') OR (CPF_CNPJ_ContraParte = '00000000000000')
  THEN CodigoFPR = '22110050';
END;
IF CodigoFPR = '22150075' THEN DO;
  IF (CdVeiculoLegal = 14273) OR (SUBSTR(NuObjetoNegociacao,1,3) = '611') OR (CPF_CNPJ_ContraParte = '00000000000000')
  THEN CodigoFPR = '22160075';
END;


IF CodigoFPR = '22170150' THEN DO;
  IF (CdVeiculoLegal = 14273) OR (SUBSTR(NuObjetoNegociacao,1,3) = '611') OR (CPF_CNPJ_ContraParte = '00000000000000')
  THEN CodigoFPR = '22180150';
END;


IF MisDate = '202506' THEN DO;
IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_PDD_TRADE') AND DLO = '530.20' THEN DO;
codigoFPR = '22070040';
END;
END;


IF MisDate = '202509' THEN DO;
	IF DataSource IN ('CORRECAO-SICQ') AND DLO = '570.10' AND NuObjetoNegociacao IN ('10061288-11' '10069311-14') THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '44000100';
	END;
	IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_SICQ-FATOCONTA') AND DLO = '530.07' THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '22060040';
	END;
	IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_SICQ-FATOCONTA') AND DLO = '570.10' THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '22070040';
	END;
	IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_RPDD') AND DLO = '526.01' THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '22060040';
	END;
	IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_ARBITRAGEM') AND DLO = '530.07' THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '22070040';
	END;
	IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_PDD_SBS') AND DLO = '530.20' THEN DO;
	codigoFPR = '22070040';
	END;

END;


IF MisDate = '202508' THEN DO;
IF DataSource IN ('CORRECAO-SICQ') AND DLO = '530.70' AND NuObjetoNegociacao IN ('10380586' '10391499' '10401717') THEN DO;
codigoFPR = '44000100';
END;

IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_ARBITRAGEM') AND DLO = '530.07' THEN DO;
codigoFPR = '22070040';
END;

IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_PDD_SBS') AND DLO = '530.20' THEN DO;
codigoFPR = '22070040';
END;

IF DataSource IN ('AJUSTE_PDD_COFIANCA') AND DLO = '570.10' THEN DO;
codigoFPR = '22070040';
END;

END;

RUN;

DATA EXTRACAO.BASE_IF_ATUAL_&_data_referencia_.;
SET BASE_IF_04;
RUN;

DATA CUBO_MARCACAO_10;
	SET BASE_DEMAIS_03 BASE_IF_04;
RUN;

		SELECT T.VrSaldoUtilizado, T.Tipo

;
